@-ms-keyframes popin {
  0% {
    -ms-transform: scale(0);
    transform: scale(0);
    opacity: 0;
  }

  85% {
    -ms-transform: scale(1.05);
    transform: scale(1.05);
    opacity: 1;
  }

  100% {
    -ms-transform: scale(1);
    transform: scale(1);
    opacity: 1;
  }
}
@keyframes popin {
  0% {
    -webkit-transform: scale(0);
    -ms-transform: scale(0);
    transform: scale(0);
    opacity: 0;
  }

  85% {
    -webkit-transform: scale(1.05);
    -ms-transform: scale(1.05);
    transform: scale(1.05);
    opacity: 1;
  }

  100% {
    -webkit-transform: scale(1);
    -ms-transform: scale(1);
    transform: scale(1);
    opacity: 1;
  }
}</style></head>

Our graph is co-op’d from the brands that opt-in to our identity network. The result is one of the largest proprietary in-market data sources available <strong>(200M+ monthly shoppers)</strong>.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once you know who a visitor is, we maintain the integrations into your CRM, ESP, Call Center, and DMP so you can activate campaigns directly inside your current workflows or use our engagement suite if you prefer.</p></div></div><div class="sqs-block button-block sqs-block-button" data-block-type="53" id="block-d363cb8467be29e2545a"><div class="sqs-block-content" id="yui_3_17_2_1_1572034571781_227"><div class="sqs-block-button-container--left" data-alignment="left" data-animation-tier="2" data-button-size="medium" id="yui_3_17_2_1_1572034571781_226">

Create data-driven customer journeys.</p></div></div><div class="sqs-block button-block sqs-block-button" data-block-type="53" id="block-7e23c141e413d13c065c"><div class="sqs-block-content" id="yui_3_17_2_1_1572034571781_243"><div class="sqs-block-button-container--left" data-alignment="left" data-animation-tier="2" data-button-size="medium" id="yui_3_17_2_1_1572034571781_242">

</div></div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block spacer-block sqs-block-spacer sized vsize-1" data-block-type="21" id="block-8b2bf3290b0436ff9f6c"><div class="sqs-block-content"> </div></div><div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code" data-block-type="23" id="block-151c52127c8f75ea635b"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="full-width-hr"></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code" data-block-type="23" id="block-851d878da72d24c259cd"><div class="sqs-block-content"><style>

<h2 class="text-align-center">Network Averages</h2>
<div class="row sqs-row">
<div class="col sqs-col-4 span-4">
<div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code">
<div class="sqs-block-content">
<div class="dis-tbl stat-block">
<div class="dis-tblcell">
<h3>25%+</h3>
<span>Open Rates</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col sqs-col-4 span-4">
<div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code">
<div class="sqs-block-content">
<div class="dis-tbl stat-block">
<div class="dis-tblcell">
<h3>26%+</h3>
<span>Click-through Rates</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col sqs-col-4 span-4">
<div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code">
<div class="sqs-block-content">
<div class="dis-tbl stat-block">
<div class="dis-tblcell">
<h3>9%+</h3>
<span>Conversion Rates</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div><div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code" data-block-type="23" id="block-a16128fc1c9b8b5cd1e9"><div class="sqs-block-content"><style>
  .result-wrap {
    background-image: url(https://www.addshoppers.com/assets/imgs/graphbg.svg);
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    background-position: bottom center;
    padding-bottom: 200px;
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
    margin-top: -150px;
  }
</style>
<div class="fullWidth-outer-heading">
<div class="fullWidth-inner midpage">
<div class="slim text-align-center">
<p><em>"By creating this <a href="https://www.addshoppers.com/blog/client-spotlight-sun-ski-sports-leverages-buzzfeed-style-quiz-to-amp-up-engagement-list-growth-with-addshoppers">fun and relaxed experience for the consumer</a> using AddShoppers, we’ve nurtured them towards taking the next step in the consumer lifecycle with our brand by having planted a positive experience and increased brand recognition."</em></p>
<img src="https://www.addshoppers.com/assets/imgs/tiffany.png"/>
<p><b>Tiffany Mass, Social Media Specialist at Sun &amp; Ski Sports</b></p>
<img src="https://www.addshoppers.com/assets/imgs/sunski-logo2.jpg"/>
</div>
</div>
</div></div></div><div class="sqs-block spacer-block sqs-block-spacer sized vsize-1" data-block-type="21" id="block-3728cceaf4c96a489ad9"><div class="sqs-block-content"> </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" id="block-ba6f4d3b02030e447159"><div class="sqs-block-content"><h2 style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;">Commerce Marketing Resources</h2><p class="" style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;">Learn how to bring your marketing into the next generation with our library of data-driven guides, white papers, and tutorials. #learnfaster</p></div></div><div class="sqs-block summary-v2-block sqs-block-summary-v2" data-block-json='{"collectionId":"53221cd4e4b0cb408d3c2023","design":"autogrid","headerText":"","textSize":"small","pageSize":6,"imageAspectRatio":"Auto","columnWidth":256,"gutter":25,"listImageSize":30,"listImageAlignment":"left","slidesPerRow":3,"textAlignment":"left","showTitle":true,"showThumbnail":true,"showExcerpt":false,"showReadMoreLink":false,"showPrice":true,"productQuickViewEnabled":false,"showPastOrUpcomingEvents":"upcoming","metadataPosition":"below-content","primaryMetadata":"cats","secondaryMetadata":"none","filter":{},"autoCrop":true,"lightbox":false,"mixedContent":true,"blockId":"22840180f9ac2cba602e"}' data-block-type="55" id="block-ebd1469adcbffe965288"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<!-- unblur cookie cache -->
<script>


    function getBlurCookie(c_name){
        var c_value = document.cookie;
        var c_start = c_value.indexOf(" " + c_name + "=");
        if (c_start == -1){
            c_start = c_value.indexOf(c_name + "=");
        }
        if (c_start == -1){
            c_value = null;
        } else {
            c_start = c_value.indexOf("=", c_start) + 1;
            var c_end = c_value.indexOf(";", c_start);
            if (c_end == -1){
                c_end = c_value.length;
            }
            c_value = unescape(c_value.substring(c_start,c_end));
        }
        return c_value;
    }

    function setBlurCookie(c_name,value,exdays){
        var exdate=new Date();
        exdate.setDate(exdate.getDate() + exdays);
        var c_value=escape(value) + ((exdays==null) ? "" : "; expires="+exdate.toUTCString());
        document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + "; " + expires + ";path=/";
    }

    function checkBlurCookie(){
        var lastTimeHere=getBlurCookie("userBlur");
        if (lastTimeHere!=null && lastTimeHere!=""){
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    var unblur = checkBlurCookie();
    if(unblur){
        $( "#block-yui_3_17_2_1_1402945012118_34979" ).hide();
        $( "#reveal-content" ).removeClass( "blur-text", 1000);
    }
</script>
